- **pca-analyzer**: Dimensionality reduction technique using Principal Component Analysis. Extracts key features, removes multicollinearity, and visualizes high-dimensional data. ([SPIRAL-EDWIN/MCM-ICM-2601000](https://github.com/SPIRAL-EDWIN/MCM-ICM-2601000/tree/HEAD/%E6%AF%94%E8%B5%9B%E6%9C%9F%E9%97%B4-26B/.github/skills/pca-analyzer) · 2 copies)
- **pca-based-clustering-evaluation**: Use when you need to demonstrate that imputation or preprocessing improves cell-subpopulation separation in scRNA-seq count matrices, by comparing PCA-based cluster compactness of raw vs imputed data against a known ground-truth clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-based-clustering-evaluation))
- **pca-based-dimensionality-reduction-of-windowed-profiles**: Use when each sample contributes a windowed multi-variable profile over age (e.g., epigenomic/ATAC-seq or RNA-seq features in moving windows spanning ~10-20 years) and a multivariate test such as MANOVA needs a compact, low-dimensional representation of each window before testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-based-dimensionality-reduction-of-windowed-profiles))
- **pca-based-drift-diagnostics**: Use when a feature matrix contains ordered pooled/intrastudy QC injections and you need to quantify or visually assess signal drift, or to verify that LOESS batch correction reduced time-related systematic variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-based-drift-diagnostics))
- **pca-based-sample-outlier-detection**: Use when a raw metabolite-by-sample matrix (e.g. Nightingale NMR or Metabolon UPLC-MS/MS export) is being QC-filtered in metaboprep and samples with abnormal multivariate metabolite profiles must be flagged via PCA scores at a user-defined SD cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-based-sample-outlier-detection))
- **pca-based-tissue-region-classification**: Use when you have a preprocessed, TIC-normalised MSI feature matrix (imzML-derived lipid intensities) and want to spatially separate tissue regions with high spectral similarity via principal components analysis and interpret driving lipids from PC loadings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-based-tissue-region-classification))
- **pca-batch-effect-assessment**: Use when you need to judge whether a metabolomics normalization method (e.g. tGAM, QC-RLSC) removed batch effects before/after correction, or to spot subpar normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-batch-effect-assessment))
- **pca-batch-effect-visualization**: Use when a raw feature table with periodic pooled QC injections (e.g. every 5–8 samples) has been drift-corrected and you need to demonstrate batch-effect removal before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-batch-effect-visualization))
- **pca-batch-runner**: PCAT 批量扫描编排：无人值守批量执行补丁兼容性分析（如 PCAT-Bench-220 全量评测）。当用户要求批量扫描、批量分析、跑一批补丁、跑评测集、跑基准、复现 220 例指标、batch scan、batch manifest、PCAT-Bench 评测时使用。负责清单生成、预检、后台启动调度器、进度汇报、断点恢复与结果聚合；不负责单个补丁的分析流程本身（由每用例独立子 Agent 会话内的 patch-compatibility-analysis 完成）。 ([neowhy9853/hyl](https://github.com/neowhy9853/hyl/tree/HEAD/%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6%E5%86%85%E5%AE%B9%E4%B8%89/patch-compatibility-utils/pca-batch-runner) · ★2)
- **pca-cell-embedding-feature-set-sweep**: Use when an scRNA-seq pipeline must decide how many top-ranked feature genes (DUBStepR-ordered) to retain before PCA and clustering, and cell type annotations may not exist for tuning that number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-cell-embedding-feature-set-sweep))
- **pca-celltype-visualization**: Use when evaluating whether a denoising method preserved or recovered celltype structure in scRNA-seq data with known labels, by comparing PCA embeddings of denoised vs. ground-truth matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-celltype-visualization))
- **pca-class-separation-statistical-testing**: Decision support for statistically evaluating whether metabolomic sample groups separate in a PCA scores plot when no univariate statistics or graphical diagnostics are available in the workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-class-separation-statistical-testing))
- **pca-clustering-alignment-verification**: Use when validating that simulated bulk RNA-seq/ATAC-seq count matrices reproduce the intended temporal expression patterns and experimental design before downstream GRN or correlation assessments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pca-clustering-alignment-verification))
- **pca-clustering-validation**: Use when you have clustered simulated (or ground-truthed) feature profiles with k-means/k-medoids and need an unsupervised confirmation that cluster assignments reflect the known simulation design before computing incorrect-assignment rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-clustering-validation))
- **pca-component-gene-ranking**: Use when benchmarking a periodicity- or trajectory-based gene ranking against a linear PCA baseline on the same scRNA-seq matrix, e.g., to test whether circular pseudo-time captures cell-cycle biology better than principal components. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-component-gene-ranking))
- **pca-data-quality-tracking**: Use when a pooled/intrastudy QC-injected LC-MS batch (e.g. NIST candidate RM 8231 / SRM 1950 plasma in mzML with a Pandas feature table) must be checked for drift, outliers, or group separation via PCA score plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-data-quality-tracking))
- **pca-decomposition**: Reduce dimensionality of multivariate data using PCA with varimax rotation. Use when you have many correlated variables and need to identify underlying factors or reduce collinearity. - **pca-denoising-malinowski-criterion**: Use when processing in-vivo CEST Z-spectral imaging data (e.g., MTRasym(3.5 ppm) APTw maps) that are too noisy for reliable voxel-wise analysis and need noise reduction before MTRasym computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/pca-denoising-malinowski-criterion))
- **pca-dimension-reduction-metabolomics**: Use when the analysis goal is unsupervised exploration of a samples x metabolites (or proteins) matrix — e.g. the ST000284 targeted metabolomics three-group study — to reduce its dimensions and visualize sample separation before supervised modelling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-dimension-reduction-metabolomics))
- **pca-dimension-reduction-of-count-matrices**: Use when a raw gene-by-cell count matrix needs dimension reduction before distance- or similarity-based steps such as scImpute's outlier-cell detection via nearest-neighbor distances. - **pca-dimension-reduction-of-scrna-counts**: Use when you have a genes-by-cells scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. GSE45719, 268 mouse embryo cells) and need low-dimensional coordinates for downstream spectral clustering or comparison of imputation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimension-reduction-of-scrna-counts))
- **pca-dimension-reduction-plotting**: Use when a metabolomics data frame plus demographic/metadata table is loaded in R and you need unsupervised sample-embedding plots (PCA, t-SNE) or a confounder/source-of-variation assessment before classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-dimension-reduction-plotting))
- **pca-dimension-reduction-single-cell**: Use when a single-cell CITE-seq or SMAGE-seq count matrix needs a linear low-dimensional embedding before UMAP visualization, clustering, or as SMOPCA's downstream input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pca-dimension-reduction-single-cell))
- **pca-dimensionality-reducer**: Apply PCA correctly for dimensionality reduction, visualisation, decorrelation, compression and monitoring: standardise first (or justify covariance PCA), choose the number of components with cumulative explained variance, scree/elbow, Kaiser's rule and parallel analysis, interpret components through signed loadings, use PCA inside scikit-learn pipelines without leakage (PCA, IncrementalPCA, TruncatedSVD for sparse/text data, KernelPCA, whitening, randomized solver), and detect multivariate outliers and process faults with Hotelling's T² and Q (SPE) residuals and per-feature contributions. - **pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when performing PCA principal component dimensionality reduction on tabular numeric data. Supports command-line parameter input, automatic numeric feature selection, parameter validation, result directory creation, and CSV or TXT format result export. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Data%20Analysis/pca-dimensionality-reduction) · ★2.1k · 3 copies)
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a metabolomics or 'omics feature table (samples × metabolites, e.g. 90 NMR-quantified metabolites across 3–8 biological replicates per cell population) needs unbiased dimensionality reduction and unsupervised visualization of sample-group clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when spot-level image (or other) features are high-dimensional vectors (e.g., 2048-d CNN outputs per spatial transcriptomics spot) and a low-dimensional latent space is needed before feeding an MLP or fusion model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a cell-by-ontology-type score matrix (e.g. pred_Y_all from OnClassModel.Predict) or another high-dimensional per-cell feature table and need a low-dimensional embedding for clustering-quality benchmarks such as silhouette analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction--ed120e73b2e0b085))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when evaluating imputation or preprocessing effects on single-cell clustering: after obtaining a gene-by-cell matrix (raw or imputed), reduce it with 50-component PCA prior to k-means clustering and ARI computation. - **pca-dimensionality-reduction-feature-extraction**: Compresses high-dimensional feature spaces into principal components using Principal Component Analysis (PCA). Use when reducing multicollinearity and visualizing complex multivariate data. ([Bishnucd/AI-skill-library](https://github.com/Bishnucd/AI-skill-library/tree/HEAD/skills/data-analytics/analysis/pca-dimensionality-reduction-feature-extraction))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-for-scrna-seq**: Use when a raw single-cell counts matrix (csv/rds/h5, genes as rows and cells as columns) must be reduced to a lower-dimensional gene space before cell clustering or neural-network cell-type annotation in a cancer TME scRNA-seq pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-for-scrna-seq))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-binned-spectral-features**: Use when you have ≥30 MS/MS spectra per compound from spectral libraries (e.g. NIST 17, MoNA) with instrument-type and collision-energy metadata and want to inspect whether spectra cluster by acquisition conditions before training a fingerprint model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-binned-spectral-features))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-count-matrices**: Use when a normalized or scaled cell-by-gene count matrix of barcoded cells needs a compact PC representation (e.g., npc = 25) to feed nearest-neighbor graph construction for ClonoCluster hybrid clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-count-matrices))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-expression-matrices**: Use when comparing dropout-imputation methods on scRNA-seq count matrices and you need a low-dimensional embedding for PCA/t-SNE visualization and spectral clustering against known cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-expression-matrices))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-sc-rna-seq-counts**: Use when a high-dimensional normalized scRNA-seq count matrix (genes x cells) must be embedded into a low-dimensional PC score space so per-PC mixed models can be fit for downstream cell-type distance estimation or inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-of-sc-rna-seq-counts))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-preprocessing**: Use when raw Tabula Muris FACS scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. 00_facs_raw_data/FACS/{Tissue}-counts.csv from the Figshare deposit) must be converted into a numeric 50-PC feature matrix as input to embedding methods such as EMBEDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-preprocessing))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scchipsq**: Use when a quality-controlled, sparse scChIP-seq count matrix (e.g. H3K27me3 from PDX tumor micro-environment samples) needs a dense low-dimensional feature space before clustering or 2D visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scchipsq))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna**: Use when a scRNA-seq preprocessing pipeline needs a compact PCA embedding (e.g., PC1-30) from scaled expression data before cell clustering, doublet simulation, or PC-count parameters for tools like DoubletFinder within Chord. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna--c895d9e6c25e5a7e))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna**: Use when a scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., AnnData .h5ad) has been QC-filtered and normalized and you need a compact latent representation for clustering or UMAP visualization, such as reproducing HuBMAP spleen cluster counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrna--260081bc53a44a5a))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrnaseq**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq expression matrix (tall or wide format, e.g. CSV/Parquet) must be projected to a low-dimensional space to enable scalable clustering on large cell counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-scrnaseq))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell**: Use when a sparse single-cell epigenomic count matrix (reads-in-peaks scATAC-seq or scChIP-seq bins) has passed QC filtering and normalization and a low-dimensional embedding is needed for clustering or 2D visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq annotation pipeline has cells rejected as 'unassigned' after marker counting and needs a low-dimensional embedding (PCA) plus clustering to reassign them by cluster similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-single-cell))
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-unsupervised**: Use when you have a feature-by-sample matrix (rows = annotated chemical features such as m/z, retention time, GNPS spectral library matches; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-unsupervised) · 2 copies)
- **pca-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when after a differential-expression test on background-subtracted microarray data, you need a low-dimensional (PCA) visualization to confirm that samples cluster by response class using only the significant genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-dimensionality-reduction-visualization))
- **pca-dmd-neural-dynamics-reconstruction**: PCA-DMD for neural dynamics reconstruction. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/pca-dmd-neural-dynamics-reconstruction) · ★4)
- **pca-embedding-batch-assessment**: Use when you need to quantify residual batch effects after correction (e.g. ComBat on two-technical-replicate mESC data) in a linear PC space, or to build the PCA baseline needed for a sensitivity comparison against a nonlinear embedding such as BEENE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-batch-assessment))
- **pca-embedding-of-deviation-scores**: Use when you need a low-dimensional cell embedding from scATAC-seq accessibility counts for clustering and want to compare it against alternatives such as SnapATAC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-of-deviation-scores))
- **pca-embedding-of-trajectory-data**: Use when pseudotime has been inferred with redPATH's consensus Hamiltonian path algorithm and you need to detect and visualize whether the developmental trajectory is linear or branched. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-embedding-of-trajectory-data))
- **pca-embedding-visualization**: Use when a trained deep metabolomics model (e.g. DenseNet121 on raw LC-MS .npy spectra) must be inspected layer-by-layer to show how hidden representations shed batch information and gain disease-class information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-embedding-visualization))

It tests models in autonomous driving, domestic assistance, and game-playing environments. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PCA-EVAL, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Action Score. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pca-eval) · ★3)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-feature-representation-extraction))
- **pca-glasso-network-overlay**: Use when high-dimensional longitudinal metabolomics data (e.g., NMR-derived metabolite concentrations across developmental stages) must be decomposed into transition-specific covariance subnetworks linking metabolites to specific cell-type transitions. - **pca-gmm-clustering-of-scrnaseq**: Use when a marker-count-based cell type identifier leaves many cells unassigned and you need to reassign them by within-cluster expression similarity, or when reproducing the 0.1-0.9 rejection/reassignment threshold sweep. - **pca-hotelling-t2-outlier-detection**: Use when a metabolomics/lipidomics data table with QCpool samples measured at regular sequence intervals must be screened for outlying samples before downstream analysis, i.e. during the 'removal of outlying samples' step of a multistep QC protocol. - **pca-input-formatting-for-trajectory-inference**: Use when preparing PC-reduced single-cell omics matrices (scRNA-seq, scATAC-seq chromVAR k-mer or TF-motif z-scores) as input to VIA's via_wrapper, or when testing trajectory-inference robustness across PC counts. - **pca-kmeans-cell-type-initialization**: Use when running DIEM on 10X droplet-based snRNA-seq (or scRNA-seq) count data and you need to initialize k cell-type clusters via PCA and k-means before run_em debris-score estimation and call_targets filtering. - **pca-latent-factor-detection**: Use when a scRNA-Seq expression matrix shows within-cell-type heterogeneity suspected to be a cell-cycle effect, and you need to test which principal components are cell-cycle driven before removing them while preserving cell-type signal. - **pca-latent-space-coordinate-scoring**: Use when you have normalized single-cell feature expression (protein counts, RNA, accessibility) plus a reduced-dimensional latent space (e.g., 50 protein-based PCs stored in an AnnData object) and need clustering-independent prediction of differentially active features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-latent-space-coordinate-scoring))
- **pca-latent-space-embedding**: Use when a library-normalized (scaled, non-log-transformed) genes x cells expression matrix must be embedded into a low-dimensional latent space before building a KNN graph or PhyloVision report, especially when cell counts exceed available memory (VISION 'pool' defaults to 'auto', micropooling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-latent-space-embedding))
- **pca-linear-baseline-ordering**: Use when benchmarking a circular/periodic pseudo-time method against a linear baseline: scRNA-seq count matrices where cells should be ordered along a latent process and you need PCA-derived PC1/PC2 orderings for comparison (e.g. GSEA NES ranking as in Fig. 4a). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-linear-baseline-ordering))
- **pca-linear-embedding-baseline-construction**: Use when you need a standard linear embedding baseline in PC space to benchmark a learned nonlinear embedding's sensitivity for batch effect estimation metrics like kBET or iLISI on single-cell RNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-linear-embedding-baseline-construction))
- **pca-loading-biomarker-prioritization**: Use when an untargeted DI-MS or LC-MS peak table has been normalized in MetaboShiny (filtered, normalized, transformed, scaled) and you need to rank which m/z values separate a disease group from controls before annotating candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loading-biomarker-prioritization))
- **pca-loading-interpretation-for-bmi-variance**: Use when you have simulated or measured 1D 1H NMR spectra of blood mixtures tied to a continuous outcome such as BMI, and need to determine which principal component captures the outcome-related variance and which metabolites drive it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loading-interpretation-for-bmi-variance))
- **pca-loading-nightingale-chart-rendering**: Use when a user has a multivariate dataset (e.g. astronomical catalogs like SDSS supernova host properties, mock galaxy catalogs, or ZENS) and needs a visual read-out of which variables drive each principal component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/pca-loading-nightingale-chart-rendering))
- **pca-loading-versus-embedding-metagene-comparison**: Use when a gene-embedding workflow has identified a candidate metagene (e.g. an IFIT1/ISG15/OAS1 cluster in interferon beta-stimulated PBMCs) and you need to demonstrate whether the program is cell-type-independent or an artifact of linear-model loading structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-loading-versus-embedding-metagene-comparison))
- **pca-loadings-and-explained-variance-interpretation**: Use when a small, feature-rich metabolomics or omics table (samples x metabolites) needs unbiased dimensionality reduction and you must inspect per-component loadings, sample scores, and explained variance to judge which components capture significant variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loadings-and-explained-variance-interpretation))
- **pca-loadings-lipid-interpretation**: Use when mass spectrometry imaging data (imzML) has been extracted, deisotoped, annotated and TIC-normalised, and the goal is to separate spatially co-located tissue regions (e.g. cerebellum layers) or interpret which lipids drive each principal component. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-loadings-lipid-interpretation))
- **pca-model-parameter-configuration**: Use when building a struct model object in R (e.g. PCA from struct/structToolbox) and you need a typed, documented, scalar-constrained parameter such as number_components, accessible via P$number_components and param_obj(P,'number_components'). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-model-parameter-configuration))
- **pca-multinomial-circadian-time-classification**: Use when you have bulk or pseudobulk transcriptomic data with known circadian timestamps for training and need to predict the circadian time of single, untimestamped samples across assay platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-multinomial-circadian-time-classification))
- **pca-neighborhood-distance-computation**: Use when running scDEED and you need the pre-embedding neighbor distances against which 2D t-SNE/UMAP neighbor distances will be correlated — i.e., after RunPCA on a Seurat object and after generating the Permuted null (row-shuffled scale.data) PCA space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-neighborhood-distance-computation))
- **pca-ordination-procrustes-analysis**: Use when comparing imputed vs original data matrices to quantify distortion of the observation/sample distribution, e.g., benchmarking MNAR imputation methods (GSimp, QRILC, kNN-TN) across graded missingness levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-ordination-procrustes-analysis))
- **pca-outlier-detection-and-missing-value-replacement**: Use when curating or reproducing QC filtering of GCxGC-TOFMS plasma metabolite peak tables — specifically when deciding which detected peaks to exclude (run-order drift, QC deviation, PCA outliers) and which metabolites need missing-value replacement, as in the 691→195 peak filter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-outlier-detection-and-missing-value-replacement))
- **pca-outlier-screening**: Use when you have a matrix of NMR-estimated metabolite relative concentrations across plasma samples (e.g. NMRQNet CSV output) and need to detect outlier samples prior to supervised group-difference modeling (OPLS-DA, t-tests). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-outlier-screening))
- **pca-population-structure-analysis**: Use when genome-wide biallelic SNP data (VCF or PLINK BED/BIM/FAM) need population structure inference via PCA and ADMIXTURE, including determining the optimal ancestral components K by CV error. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/pca-population-structure-analysis))
- **pca-ppr-generator-xtal**: Motor gerador do Programa de Conservação Auditiva (PCA) e Programa de Proteção Respiratória (PPR) (NR-07 / NR-09). ([clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS](https://github.com/clodoaldoperito-droid/ARQUITETURA_JARVIS/tree/HEAD/agent_skills/pca-ppr-generator-xtal) · ★1)
- **pca-preprocessing-of-single-cell-data**: Use when a scRNA-seq expression matrix has thousands of features (e.g. ~23,686 genes) and must be scaled and compressed to PCA-50 prior to training ivis (k=15, model='maaten') for 2D embedding. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-preprocessing-of-single-cell-data))
- **pca-quality-control-of-nmr-spectra**: Use when QC of a large-scale 1H NMR metabonomic dataset (e.g. plasma spectra with pooled QC replicates) is required before metabolome-wide association analysis, to verify analytical reproducibility and absence of batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-quality-control-of-nmr-spectra))
- **pca-quality-control-tightness-evaluation**: Use when a corrected metabolomics feature matrix (e.g., post-WaveICA or WaveICA 2.0) must be scored for batch effect removal quality and pooled QCS injections are available to benchmark clustering tightness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-quality-control-tightness-evaluation))
- **pca-reconstruction-error-rejection**: Use when an unlabeled single-cell test set may contain cell populations absent from a labeled training set, and you need to reject (rather than force-classify) cells before hierarchical classification with scHPL. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-reconstruction-error-rejection))
- **pca-reserve-analyzer**: Converts a Property Condition Assessment (PCA) into an underwriting-ready capital plan: immediate (Year 0) repair costs, a 10-year replacement-reserve schedule by building component, an implied per-unit-per-year (PUPY) or per-SF reserve, a reserve-adequacy gap versus the lender-underwritten reserve, prioritized diligence issues, and IC questions. Triggers on 'PCA', 'property condition assessment', 'replacement reserve', 'reserve adequacy', 'PNA', or when a property condition report needs to be turned into capital numbers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/pca-reserve-analyzer) · ★52)
- **pca-score-based-mid-level-fusion**: Use when two or more platform-specific metabolomics datasets (e.g., MS and NMR CSV tables) covering the same samples must be combined into one integrated table and raw column concatenation would duplicate correlated metabolite information across platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-based-mid-level-fusion))
- **pca-score-extraction**: Use when multivariate data in a struct DatasetExperiment needs mean centring followed by PCA, and you must extract principal-component scores (e.g. number_components = 2) as the pipeline output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-extraction))
- **pca-score-interpretation-in-networks**: Use when you have both (1) a sparse network graph from GLASSO showing conditional dependencies among variables, and (2) PCA scores representing variance or loadings in a lower-dimensional space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/pca-score-interpretation-in-networks))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a targeted LC–MS preprocessing run (e.g., TARDIS AUC table) needs a global quality check, and you want to confirm QC runs cluster tightly before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--ef2196d89bfd4869))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a grouped NMR peak feature matrix (samples × peaks) must be checked for class structure: Pareto-scale and mean-center it, run PCA, and inspect the PC1–PC2 score plot colored by wine color for red vs white/rosé separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--d3f922e29b3e4527))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization**: Use when a LipidomicsExperiment (e.g. imported from Metabolomics Workbench study ST001111 with measure="Area") needs unsupervised quality control, and samples with significantly large dispersion in the PCA scores plot must be identified as outliers before further analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization--bd8df5891cc930c0))
- **pca-score-plot-visualization-of-multi-tissue-data**: Use when you have untargeted metabolomics peak-area tables with many zero-count features across multiple tissues and need to check whether samples cluster by study, cohort, genotype, or tissue before downstream stratified analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-score-plot-visualization-of-multi-tissue-data))
- **pca-scores-interpretation-metabolomics**: Use when you have a peak-value feature matrix from 1D NMR metabolomics spectra and need to visually assess whether experimental groups (e.g., diet concentrations) separate before or alongside differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-interpretation-metabolomics))
- **pca-scores-loadings-export**: Use when you have SMolESY-select relative concentration values for the 22 SMolESY-select metabolites and want to verify whether PCA of the panel recapitulates the biomarker signal seen when analysing the full 1H NMR profile. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-loadings-export))
- **pca-scores-significance-testing**: Use when a cleaned, class-labelled X data matrix (DSO with row/column labels and biological/blank class labels) is ready and you need PCA with per-component statistical evidence of class separation rather than visual scores plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scores-significance-testing))
- **pca-scree-biplot-generation**: Use when FT-ICR MS metabolomic samples with assigned molecular formulas need ordination-based group discrimination assessment, complementing PERMANOVA and NMDS, using normalized peak intensities or molecular class composition vectors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-scree-biplot-generation))
- **pca-signal-equivalence-verification**: Use when reconstructing scLENS's RMT noise-filtering module and you must prove that eigen-decomposition of the cell similarity matrix G = XX^T/N yields a signal subspace equivalent to PCA scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pca-signal-equivalence-verification))
- **pca-skill-manager**: Acelera a criacao e manutencao de skills do Claude. Automatiza o scaffolding de pastas, validacao de metadados YAML e empacotamento ZIP correto. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-agents/pca-skill-manager) · ★67 · 3 copies)
- **pca-test-entry-generator**: 根据最终确认的 Patch Compatibility Analysis findings，生成或细化可运行的测试入口命令、复现程序或项目原生测试代码。在 patch-compatibility-testing 已校验 analysis.json 并生成 test-entry-tasks.json 后使用。 ([neowhy9853/hyl](https://github.com/neowhy9853/hyl/tree/HEAD/%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6%E5%86%85%E5%AE%B9%E4%B8%89/patch-compatibility-utils/patch-compatibility-testing/pca-test-entry-generator) · ★2)
- **pca-transcriptional-similarity-bin-assignment**: Use when a binned Visium HD AnnData object carries nucleus/cell labels (from bin2cell.insert_labels and bin2cell.expand_labels) and each 2 μm bin must be assigned to its final cell, with ambiguous equidistant bins resolved by gene-expression similarity. - **pca-tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when the input is an imzML MSI dataset whose high-dimensional pixel spectra must be reduced to a 2D embedding (PCA→t-SNE) for clustering-based tissue segmentation and hyperparameter comparison. - **pca-tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when you have an imputed single-cell expression matrix and known cell-type labels, and need a 2D embedding (t-SNE after PCA) to visually and quantitatively check cell-type separation. - **pca-umap-cell-embedding-projection**: Use when comparing synthetic scATAC-seq cells to real cells at the cell-embedding level: you have peak-by-cell count matrices for real and simulated cells (BAM/BED-derived) and need a quantitative mixing score such as miLISI. - **pca-variance-explanation-lipidomics**: Use when an annotated lipidomics feature table (e.g., 3647 features across replicate groups like Par, Dap2, Dal2, Van4) must be reduced to a few components to test whether strain or condition clusters separate before downstream PLS-DA or fold-change analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/pca-variance-explanation-lipidomics))
- **pca-variance-structure-comparison**: Use when two quantification pipelines have been run on the same RNA-seq FASTQ samples and you need to demonstrate equivalence by checking whether normalized count tables yield near-identical PCA variance structures and comparable DEG counts. - **pca-versus-zinbwave-dimensionality-reduction-comparison**: Use when raw scRNA-seq count matrices must be reduced to a low-dimensional space for clustering or trajectory inference, and dropouts/over-dispersion may make PCA representations miss lineage endpoints or cell types. - **pca-visualization-of-imputed-expression**: Use when comparing raw vs. imputed count matrices (Bfimpute, SCRABBLE, netNMF-sc, etc.) and you need a visualization-level and cell-relationship-level assessment of whether imputation improved cell-type separation. - **pca-vs-nonlinear-embedding-batch-benchmarking**: Use when quantifying batch effects in single-cell RNA-seq data where PCA-space metrics may miss nonlinear batch structure — e.g., comparing uncorrected vs. ComBat/Harmony-corrected data with kBET and iLISI. - **pca_analysis**: Run PLINK PCA on a BFILE dataset and generate a PCA variance report using the built-in pca_analysis tool and bundled scripts. ([lukegood/EasyGS](https://github.com/lukegood/EasyGS/tree/HEAD/easygs/skills/pca_analysis) · ★10)

- **pcb-design-guidelines**: 根据现有原理图或网表制定可逐步执行、落实到器件焊盘的 PCB 布局布线指南，或审查 PCB 的分区、关键回路、回流、层叠、阻抗、线宽/间距、转角、过孔、热、隔离、装配和可制造性。用户询问不同器件怎么摆、线怎么走、从哪个引脚出线或如何学习开源 PCB 时也使用。适用于 KiCad、Altium、EasyEDA 及可读的 CAD 导出；具体数值由项目约束、器件资料、计算和制造能力确定。 ([Ausanx/modular-schematic-layout](https://github.com/Ausanx/modular-schematic-layout/tree/HEAD/pcb-design-guidelines) · ★4)

Single- or double-layer polyimide; rarely multi-layer. RA copper for dynamic flex (3× cycle life vs ED). Trace perpendicular to bend axis, curved corners, cross-hatched pours, stiffeners at connectors, IPC-FC-234 cycle test. - **pcb-specialized-hdi**: HDI (High-Density Interconnect) PCB design — microvias, fine line/space, sequential lamination. - **pcb-specialized-heavy-copper**: Heavy Copper PCB design — ≥3 oz Cu, up to 20+ oz, busbar inlays. Use for high-current power distribution (motor drives, EV traction, BMS, welders, inverters). Trace / space grows with copper weight; Cu balance critical for warpage; multi-via for layer transitions; reflow profile requires extended preheat; no thermal relief on power pads. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-heavy-copper) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-high-power**: High-Power PCB design from 1 A to thousands of A — inverters, motor drives, EV chargers, UPS, SMPS, BMS, welders. Heavy copper (≥2 oz), busbars, copper inlays, and parallel vias are baseline. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-high-power) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-high-speed**: High-Speed Digital PCB design — DDR, PCIe, USB 3+, HDMI, Ethernet, LVDS, MIPI, FPGA/SoC. Use when edge rates fall below ~6× the round-trip delay of a trace; from clock frequency 100+ MHz with fast edges. Covers transmission lines, controlled impedance ($Z_0$, $Z_{diff}$), reflections, crosstalk, skew, return current, length matching, stubs/back-drilling, termination, PDN integrity, and eye-diagram simulation. Stackup with continuous reference planes is non-negotiable. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-high-speed) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-high-temp**: High-Temperature PCB design — operating ≥100 °C continuous, peak ≥175 °C. Use for engine compartment, down-hole oil/gas, aerospace engine controls, industrial drives near motors. Choose laminate by Tg + Td; derate components; no standard electrolytic; high-Tg FR-4 / polyimide / ceramic substrate. Thermal cycle qualification mandatory. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-high-temp) · ★1)

- **pcb-specialized-industrial-control**: Industrial Control PCB — PLCs, motor controllers, sensors, SCADA, RS-485/CAN/Ethernet I/O, safety relays. - **pcb-specialized-iot-wireless**: IoT / Wireless PCB design — smart home sensors, asset trackers, agricultural monitors, industrial wireless. - **pcb-specialized-isolated-psu**: Isolated Power Supply PCB — flyback, forward, push-pull, half-bridge, full-bridge, LLC resonant. Use for AC-DC adapters, isolated DC-DC, medical 2 × MOPP supplies. - **pcb-specialized-led**: LED PCB design — lighting, indicators, displays, automotive lamps, UV cure, photometry. PCB itself is part of noise budget: surface leakage, board capacitance, microphonics, thermal EMF. Guard rings, shielded cables, lock-in / chopper architectures, IPC-J-STD-001 cleanliness. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-low-noise-sensor) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-medical**: Medical PCB design — patient monitors, ECG/EEG/EMG, defibrillators, infusion pumps, implants. Use when designing patient-applied parts (B/BF/CF), MOPP / MOOP isolation barriers, low-leakage defib-protected biopotential AFEs, and IEC 60601-1 compliant safety. Patient leakage current limits (10–500 µA), 2 × MOPP for reinforced, traceable design controls per ISO 13485 / FDA 21 CFR 820. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-medical) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-metal-core**: Metal-Core / IMS (Insulated Metal Substrate) PCB design — aluminum or copper base, thin dielectric, single copper conductor layer. Use for LED arrays, automotive lighting, power modules where thermal conductivity through PCB matters. $k = 1.5–8$ W/m·K dielectric, $\theta$ ≈ 0.5 K/W per cm² typical. Single-layer routing forces jumpers; TIM at heatsink interface mandatory. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-metal-core) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-mixed-signal**: Mixed-Signal PCB — MCU + ADC + sensors + wireless + power on one board (IoT, energy meters, smart appliances). Use when partitioning analog and digital domains, managing return current across the board, deciding split-vs-solid ground plane, and routing signals between domains cleanly. Modern best practice: single GND plane + partition by component placement; splits cause more problems than they solve. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-mixed-signal) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-mmwave**: mmWave PCB design from 24 GHz to 300 GHz — 77 GHz auto radar, 60 GHz WiGig, 5G mmWave, satellite Ka-band. Use when designing patch antennas, SIW transitions, connector launches, or any mmWave routing where every µm matters. Demands low-loss laminates (Rogers RO3003, RT/duroid), HVLP/VLP-2 copper, EM-simulation-tuned launches, no vias on RF, tight tolerance, and field-solver design (HFSS/CST/Sonnet). ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-mmwave) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-motor-drive**: Motor Drive PCB design — BLDC, PMSM, induction, stepper, EV traction. Use for three-phase bridges with gate drivers, current sensing (inline / low-side / DC link / Hall), position sensing (Hall / encoder / resolver), bootstrap or isolated drivers, snubbers, FOC / sensorless control. Combines high-power + EMC + analog sensing + safety. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-motor-drive) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-power-electronics**: Power Electronics / Switching Converter PCB — buck, boost, flyback, forward, LLC, PFC, inverters. Use when designing high-di/dt switching circuits, minimizing power-loop area, placing gate drivers, planning Kelvin sense + bootstrap layout, selecting snubbers, and managing feedback-loop layout for stability. Hot-loop minimization (&lt;5 mm²) is the single most important rule. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-power-electronics) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-precision-analog**: Precision Analog PCB — energy meters, scales, ADC frontends, sensor amplifiers. Use when measuring microvolts / nanoamps / ppm accuracy: thermal noise, 1/f noise, leakage, guard rings, Kelvin sensing, ADC reference layout, anti-alias filtering. PCB layout is part of the noise budget — surface leakage, board capacitance, microphonics, thermal EMF all matter. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-precision-analog) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-rf**: RF / Microwave PCB design from 10 MHz to ~6 GHz — Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, GPS, LoRa, LTE/5G sub-6, antennas, RF amplifiers, filters. Use when designing 50 Ω signal paths, integrating antennas, designing matching networks, or routing impedance-controlled microstrip / CPWG. Covers controlled impedance with field solver, S-parameters, skin effect, dielectric loss, antenna keep-outs, π-matching networks, low-loss laminates (Rogers), and via fences. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-rf) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-rigid-flex**: Rigid-Flex PCB design — combines rigid (FR-4) and flexible (polyimide) sections in one piece. Use for compact / curved / folded products: cameras, smartphones, wearables, medical devices, aerospace. Strict bend-zone keep-out, rolled-annealed copper for dynamic bend, stiffeners at connector zones, cross-hatched ground pour in flex, IPC-FC-234 cycle qualification. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-rigid-flex) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-telecom-poe**: Telecom / PoE PCB design — routers, switches, IP cameras, access points, VoIP. Use for Ethernet 10/100/1G/10G with magnetics + RJ45 (or mag-jack), Power-over-Ethernet 802.3af/at/bt (up to 90 W), Bob Smith termination, surge / lightning protection per IEC 61000-4-5 + ITU-T K.20/21, isolated PD interface with hot-swap MOSFET, isolated DC-DC for output rails. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-telecom-poe) · ★1)
- **pcb-specialized-wearable**: Wearable PCB design — smartwatches, fitness bands, hearing aids, medical patches, AR/VR glasses. Use for compact / curved / flex form factor, low-power for multi-day battery, RF antenna detuned by body, sweat / moisture exposure, skin-contact biocompatibility, ESD on exposed contacts. HDI + rigid-flex + tight power management. ([hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills](https://github.com/hyndex/Schematics-and-PCB-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-specialized-wearable) · ★1)
- **pcb-stackup-and-impedance**: Use when designing PCB layer stackups and calculating controlled impedance — selecting materials, assigning signal/plane layers, calculating trace geometry for target Z0, and generating stackup documentation for fabrication. ([iamdarshg/pcb-skill](https://github.com/iamdarshg/pcb-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/pcb-development/pcb-stackup-and-impedance) · ★1)
- **pcb-step-slim**: Slim down a heavy PCB STEP/STP assembly so it opens fast in SolidWorks (or any CAD) for mechanical / enclosure review. Strips non-mechanical detail — copper traces, pads, vias, soldermask, silkscreen, BGA solder balls, and flat SMD chip passives — while keeping the board outline, all components, and tall/height- driving parts. Use when a STEP file is too large or too slow to open, when the user wants to "lighten / simplify / reduce / 경량화" a PCB STEP, or to measure per-component heights. Reference-closed text surgery (fast, exact geometry) with optional OpenCASCADE (pythonocc) analysis + load… ([Seobuk/pcb-step-slim](https://github.com/Seobuk/pcb-step-slim/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
